# Security Audit: Missing Authentication Middleware in HTTP Handlers

## Your Task

Find Go source files in the Loki and Mimir repositories that register HTTP handlers or routes without applying authentication middleware such as `middleware.AuthenticateUser`.

## Context

You are working on a codebase task involving repos from the security domain.

## Available Resources

The local `/workspace/` directory contains: sg-evals/grafana--26d36ec.


## Output Format

Create a file at `/workspace/answer.json` with your findings in the following structure:

```json
{
  "files": [
    {"repo": "repo-name", "path": "relative/path/to/file.go"}
  ],
  "symbols": [
    {"repo": "repo-name", "path": "relative/path/to/file.go", "symbol": "SymbolName"}
  ],
  "chain": [
    {"repo": "repo-name", "path": "relative/path/to/file.go", "symbol": "FunctionName"}
  ],
  "text": "Narrative explanation of your findings, citing repos and file paths."
}
```

Include only the fields relevant to this task. Your answer is evaluated against a closed-world oracle — completeness matters.

## Evaluation

Your answer will be scored on:
- **File recall and precision**: Did you find all relevant files?

# AGENTS.md

<!-- docs-ai-begin -->

<!-- version: 1.1.0 -->

## Documentation

Instructions for documentation authoring in Markdown files.

DOCS.md contains all the Docs AI toolkit docs in one file.

## Role

Act as an experienced software engineer and technical writer for Grafana Labs.

Write for software developers and engineers who understand general programming concepts.

Focus on practical implementation and clear problem-solving guidance.

### Grafana

Use full product names on first mention, then short names:

- Grafana Alloy (full), Alloy (short)
- Grafana Beyla (full), Beyla (short)

Use "OpenTelemetry Collector" on first mention, then "Collector" for subsequent references.
Keep full name for distributions, headings, and links.

Always use "Grafana Cloud" in full.

Use complete terms:

- "OpenTelemetry" (not "OTel")
- "Kubernetes" (not "K8s")

Present observability signals in order: metrics, logs, traces, and profiles.

Focus content on Grafana solutions when discussing integrations or migrations.

## Style

### Structure

Structure articles into sections with headings.

Leave Markdown front matter content between two triple dashes `---`.

The front matter YAML `title` and the content h1 (#) heading should be the same.
Make sure there's an h1 heading in the content; this redundancy is required.

Always include copy after a heading or between headings, for example:

```markdown
## Heading

Immediately followed by copy and not another heading.

## Sub heading
```

The immediate copy after a heading should introduce and provide an overview of what's covered in the section.

Start articles with an introduction that covers the goal of the article. Example goals:

- Learn concepts
- Set up or install something
- Configure something
- Use a product to solve a business problem
- Troubleshoot a problem
- Integrate with other software or systems
- Migrate from one thing to another
- Refer to APIs or reference documentation

Follow the goal with a list of prerequisites, for example:

```markdown
Before you begin, ensure you have the following:

- <Prerequisite 1>
- <Prerequisite 2>
- ...
```

Suggest and link to next steps and related resources at the end of the article, for example:

- Learn more about A, B, C
- Configure X
- Use X to achieve Y
- Use X to achieve Z
- Project homepage or documentation
- Project repository (for example, GitHub, GitLab)
- Project package (for example, pip or NPM)

You don't need to use the "Refer to..." syntax for next steps; use the link text directly.

### Copy

Write simple, direct copy with short sentences and paragraphs.

Use contractions:

- it's, isn't, that's, you're, don't

Choose simple words:

- use (not utilize)
- help (not assist)
- show (not demonstrate)

Write with verbs and nouns. Use minimal adjectives except when describing Grafana Labs products.

## Tense

Write in present simple tense.

Avoid present continuous tense.

Only write in future tense to show future actions.

### Voice

Always write in an active voice.

Change passive voice to active voice.

### Perspective

Address users as "you".

Use second person perspective consistently.

### Wordlist

Use allowlist/blocklist instead of whitelist/blacklist.

Use primary/secondary instead of master/slave.

Use "refer to" instead of "see", "consult", "check out", and other phrases.

### Formatting

Use sentence case for titles and headings.

Use inline Markdown links: [Link text](https://example.com).

Link to other sections using descriptive phrases that include the section name:
"For setup details, refer to the [Lists](#lists) section."

Bold text with two asterisks: **bold**

Emphasize text with one underscore: _italics_

Format UI elements using sentence case as they appear:

- Click **Submit**.
- Navigate to **User settings**.
- Configure **Alerting rules**.

### Lists

Write complete sentences for lists:

- Works with all languages and frameworks (correct)
- All languages and frameworks (incorrect)

Use dashes for unordered...

    62→
    63→func (am *LotexAM) withAMReq(
    64→	ctx *contextmodel.ReqContext,
    65→	method string,
    66→	endpoint string,
    67→	pathParams []string,
    68→	body io.Reader,
    69→	extractor func(*response.NormalResponse) (interface{}, error),
    70→	headers map[string]string,
    71→) response.Response {
    72→	datasourceUID := web.Params(ctx.Req)[":DatasourceUID"]
    73→	if datasourceUID == "" {
    74→		return response.Error(http.StatusBadRequest, "DatasourceUID is invalid", nil)
    75→	}
    76→
    77→	ds, err := am.DataProxy.DataSourceCache.GetDatasourceByUID(ctx.Req.Context(), datasourceUID, ctx.SignedInUser, ctx.SkipDSCache)
    78→	if err != nil {
    79→		if errors.Is(err, datasources.ErrDataSourceAccessDenied) {
    80→			return ErrResp(http.StatusForbidden, err, "Access denied to datasource")
    81→		}
    82→		if errors.Is(err, datasources.ErrDataSourceNotFound) {
    83→			return ErrResp(http.StatusNotFound, err, "Unable to find datasource")
    84→		}
    85→		return ErrResp(http.StatusInternalServerError, err, "Unable to load datasource meta data")
    86→	}
    87→
    88→	impl := ds.JsonData.Get("implementation").MustString(defaultImplementation)
    89→	implEndpoints, ok := endpoints[impl]
    90→	if !ok {
    91→		return ErrResp(http.StatusBadRequest, fmt.Errorf("unsupported Alert Manager implementation \"%s\"", impl), "")
    92→	}
    93→	endpointPath, ok := implEndpoints[endpoint]
    94→	if !ok {
    95→		return ErrResp(http.StatusBadRequest, fmt.Errorf("unsupported endpoint \"%s\" for Alert Manager implementation \"%s\"", endpoint, impl), "")
    96→	}
    97→
    98→	iPathParams := make([]interface{}, len(pathParams))
    99→	for idx, value := range pathParams {
   100→		iPathParams[idx] = value
   101→	}
   102→
   103→	return am.withReq(
   104→		ctx,
   105→		method,
   106→		withPath(*ctx.Req.URL, fmt.Sprintf(endpointPath, iPathParams...)),
   107→		body,
   108→		extractor,
   109→		headers,
   110→	)
   111→}

    38→type frontendService struct {
    39→	*services.BasicService
    40→	cfg          *setting.Cfg
    41→	httpServ     *http.Server
    42→	features     featuremgmt.FeatureToggles
    43→	log          log.Logger
    44→	errChan      chan error
    45→	promGatherer prometheus.Gatherer
    46→	promRegister prometheus.Registerer
    47→	tracer       trace.Tracer
    48→	license      licensing.Licensing
    49→

    51→}
    52→
    53→func ProvideFrontendService(cfg *setting.Cfg, features featuremgmt.FeatureToggles, promGatherer prometheus.Gatherer, promRegister prometheus.Registerer, license licensing.Licensing) (*frontendService, error) {
    54→	assetsManifest, err := fswebassets.GetWebAssets(cfg, license)
    55→	if err != nil {
    56→		return nil, err
    57→	}
    58→
    59→	index, err := NewIndexProvider(cfg, assetsManifest)
    60→	if err != nil {
    61→		return nil, err
    62→	}
    63→
    64→	s := &frontendService{
    65→		cfg:          cfg,
    66→		features:     features,
    67→		log:          log.New("frontend-server"),
    68→		promGatherer: promGatherer,
    69→		promRegister: promRegister,
    70→		tracer:       tracer,
    71→		license:      license,
    72→		index:        index,
    73→	}
    74→	s.BasicService = services.NewBasicService(s.start, s.running, s.stop)
    75→	return s, nil
    76→}
    77→
    78→func (s *frontendService) start(ctx context.Context) error {
    79→	s.httpServ = s.newFrontendServer(ctx)
    80→	s.errChan = make(chan error)
    81→	go func() {
    82→		s.errChan <- s.httpServ.ListenAndServe()
    83→	}()
    84→	return nil
    85→}
    86→
    87→func (s *frontendService) running(ctx context.Context) error {
    88→	select {
    89→	case <-ctx.Done():
    90→		return nil
    91→	case err := <-s.errChan:
    92→		return err
    93→	}
    94→}
    95→
    96→func (s *frontendService) stop(failureReason error) error {
    97→	s.log.Info("stopping frontend server", "reason", failureReason)
    98→
    99→	if err := s.httpServ.Shutdown(context.Background()); err != nil {
   100→		s.log.Error("failed to shutdown frontend server", "error", err)
   101→		return err
   102→	}
   103→	return nil
   104→}
   105→

Output

     1→package client
     2→
     3→import (
     4→	"net/http"
     5→)
     6→
     7→const (
     8→	MimirTenantHeader        = "X-Scope-OrgID"
     9→	RemoteAlertmanagerHeader = "X-Remote-Alertmanager"
    10→)
    11→
    12→type MimirAuthRoundTripper struct {
    13→	TenantID string
    14→	Password string
    15→	Next     http.RoundTripper
    16→}
    17→
    18→// RoundTrip implements the http.RoundTripper interface
    19→// It adds an `X-Scope-OrgID` header with the TenantID if only provided with a tenantID or sets HTTP Basic Authentication if both
    20→// a tenantID and a password are provided.
    21→func (r *MimirAuthRoundTripper) RoundTrip(req *http.Request) (*http.Response, error) {
    22→	req.Header.Set(RemoteAlertmanagerHeader, "true")
    23→	if r.TenantID != "" && r.Password == "" {
    24→		req.Header.Set(MimirTenantHeader, r.TenantID)
    25→	}
    26→
    27→	if r.TenantID != "" && r.Password != "" {
    28→		req.SetBasicAuth(r.TenantID, r.Password)
    29→	}
    30→
    31→	return r.Next.RoundTrip(req)
    32→}
    33→

    20→func GrafanaJavascriptAgentLogMessageHandler(store *frontendlogging.SourceMapStore) frontendLogMessageHandler {
    21→	return func(hs *HTTPServer, c *web.Context) {

    23→		if err := web.Bind(c.Req, &event); err != nil {
    24→			c.Resp.WriteHeader(http.StatusBadRequest)
    25→			_, err = c.Resp.Write([]byte("bad request data"))
    26→			if err != nil {
    27→				hs.log.Error("could not write to response", "err", err)
    28→			}
    29→		}
    30→
    31→		// Meta object is standard across event types, adding it globally.
    32→

    34→			for _, logEntry := range event.Logs {
    35→				var ctx = frontendlogging.CtxVector{}
    36→				ctx = event.AddMetaToContext(ctx)
    37→				ctx = append(ctx, "kind", "log", "original_timestamp", logEntry.Timestamp)
    38→
    39→				for k, v := range frontendlogging.KeyValToInterfaceMap(logEntry.KeyValContext()) {
    40→					ctx = append(ctx, k, v)
    41→				}
    42→				switch logEntry.LogLevel {
    43→				case frontendlogging.LogLevelDebug, frontendlogging.LogLevelTrace:
    44→					{
    45→						ctx = append(ctx, "original_log_level", logEntry.LogLevel)
    46→						frontendLogger.Debug(logEntry.Message, ctx...)
    47→					}
    48→				case frontendlogging.LogLevelError:
    49→					{
    50→						ctx = append(ctx, "original_log_level", logEntry.LogLevel)
    51→						frontendLogger.Error(logEntry.Message, ctx...)
    52→					}
    53→				case frontendlogging.LogLevelWarning:
    54→					{
    55→						ctx = append(ctx, "original_log_level", logEntry.LogLevel)
    56→						frontendLogger.Warn(logEntry.Message, ctx...)
    57→					}
    58→				default:
    59→					{
    60→						ctx = append(ctx, "original_log_level", logEntry.LogLevel)
    61→						frontendLogger.Info(logEntry.Message, ctx...)
    62→					}
    63→				}
    64→			}
    65→		}
    66→
    67→		if len(event.Measurements) > 0 {
    68→			for _, measurementEntry := range event.Measurements {
    69→				for measurementName, measurementValue := range measurementEntry.Values {
    70→					var ctx = frontendlogging.CtxVector{}
    71→					ctx = event.AddMetaToContext(ctx)
    72→					ctx = append(ctx, measurementName, measurementValue)
    73→					ctx = append(ctx, "kind", "measurement", "original_timestamp", measurementEntry.Timestamp)
    74→					frontendLogger.Info("Measurement: "+measurementEntry.Type, ctx...)
    75→				}
    76→			}
    77→		}
    78→		if len(event.Exceptions) > 0 {
    79→			for _, exception := range event.Exceptions {
    80→				var ctx = frontendlogging.CtxVector{}
    81→				ctx = event.AddMetaToContext(ctx)
    82→				exception := exception
    83→				transformedException := frontendlogging.TransformException(c.Req.Context(), &exception, store)
    84→				ctx = append(ctx, "kind", "exception", "type", transformedException.Type, "value", transformedException.Value, "stacktrace", transformedException.String())
    85→				ctx = append(ctx, "original_timestamp", exception.Timestamp)
    86→				frontendLogger.Error(exception.Message(), ctx...)
    87→			}
    88→		}
    89→		c.Resp.WriteHeader(http.StatusAccepted)
    90→		_, err := c.Resp.Write([]byte("OK"))
    91→		if err != nil {
    92→			hs.log.Error("could not write to response", "err", err)
    93→		}
    94→	}

    25→		return

    29→	pluginID := pathParts[len(pathParts)-1]

# AGENTS.md

<!-- docs-ai-begin -->

<!-- version: 1.1.0 -->

## Documentation

Instructions for documentation authoring in Markdown files.

DOCS.md contains all the Docs AI toolkit docs in one file.

## Role

Act as an experienced software engineer and technical writer for Grafana Labs.

Write for software developers and engineers who understand general programming concepts.

Focus on practical implementation and clear problem-solving guidance.

### Grafana

Use full product names on first mention, then short names:

- Grafana Alloy (full), Alloy (short)
- Grafana Beyla (full), Beyla (short)

Use "OpenTelemetry Collector" on first mention, then "Collector" for subsequent references.
Keep full name for distributions, headings, and links.

Always use "Grafana Cloud" in full.

Use complete terms:

- "OpenTelemetry" (not "OTel")
- "Kubernetes" (not "K8s")

Present observability signals in order: metrics, logs, traces, and profiles.

Focus content on Grafana solutions when discussing integrations or migrations.

## Style

### Structure

Structure articles into sections with headings.

Leave Markdown front matter content between two triple dashes `---`.

The front matter YAML `title` an...

   689→func (hs *HTTPServer) metricsEndpoint(ctx *web.Context) {
   690→	if !hs.Cfg.MetricsEndpointEnabled {
   691→		return
   692→	}
   693→
   694→	if ctx.Req.Method != http.MethodGet || ctx.Req.URL.Path != "/metrics" {
   695→		return
   696→	}
   697→
   698→	if hs.metricsEndpointBasicAuthEnabled() && !BasicAuthenticatedRequest(ctx.Req, hs.Cfg.MetricsEndpointBasicAuthUsername, hs.Cfg.MetricsEndpointBasicAuthPassword) {
   699→		ctx.Resp.Header().Set("WWW-Authenticate", `Basic realm="Grafana"`)
   700→		ctx.Resp.WriteHeader(http.StatusUnauthorized)
   701→		return
   702→	}
   703→
   704→	promhttp.
   705→		HandlerFor(hs.promGatherer, promhttp.HandlerOpts{EnableOpenMetrics: true}).
   706→		ServeHTTP(ctx.Resp, ctx.Req)
   707→}
   708→

   711→	notHeadOrGet := ctx.Req.Method != http.MethodGet ...

    18→
    19→func UploadErrorToStatusCode(err error) int {
    20→	switch {
    21→	case errors.Is(err, ErrStorageNotFound):
    22→		return 404
    23→
    24→	case errors.Is(err, ErrUnsupportedStorage):
    25→		return 400
    26→
    27→	case errors.Is(err, ErrValidationFailed):
    28→		return 400
    29→
    30→	case errors.Is(err, ErrQuotaReached):
    31→		return 400
    32→
    33→	case errors.Is(err, ErrFileAlreadyExists):
    34→		return 400
    35→
    36→	case errors.Is(err, ErrAccessDenied):
    37→		return 403
    38→
    39→	default:
    40→		return 500
    41→	}
    42→}

#142 assistant/tool_use Read
#143 user/tool_result Read
     1→package client
     2→
     3→import (
     4→	"net/http"
     5→)
     6→
     7→const (
     8→	MimirTenantHeader        = "X-Scope-OrgID"
     9→	RemoteAlertmanagerHeader = "X-Remote-Alertmanager"
    10→)
    11→
    12→type MimirAuthRoundTripper struct {
    13→	TenantID string
    14→	Password string
    15→	Next     http.RoundTripper
    16→}
    17→
    18→// RoundTrip implements the http.RoundTripper interface
    19→// It adds an `X-Scope-OrgID` header with the TenantID if only provided with a tenantID or sets HTTP Basic Authentication if both
    20→// a tenantID and a password are provided.
    21→func (r *MimirAuthRoundTripper) RoundTrip(req *http.Request) (*http.Response, error) {
    22→	req.Header.Set(RemoteAlertmanagerHeader, "true")
    23→	if r.TenantID != "" && r.Password == "" {
    24→		req.Header.Set(MimirTenantHeader, r.TenantID)
    25→	}
    26→
    27→	if r.TenantID != "" && r.Password != "" {
    28→		req.SetBasicAuth(r.TenantID, r.Password)
    29→	}
    30→
    31→	return r.Next.RoundTrip(req)
    32→}

- All these endpoints use `middleware.ReqSignedIn`, not `middleware.AuthenticateUser`
- Some Grafana routes are intentionally unauthenticated (login, snapshots, signup) but are unrelated to Loki/Mimir functionality

Since the actual Loki and Mimir repositories are not present in the workspace, no files matching the specific security pattern (`middleware.AuthenticateUser` missing from Loki/Mimir route registrations) could be identified.
